### [75] [BioMedJImpact: A Comprehensive Dataset and LLM Pipeline for AI Engagement and Scientific Impact Analysis of Biomedical Journals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05227)
*Ruiyu Wang,Yuzhang Xie,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang,Jiaying Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioMedJImpact，一个基于174万篇PubMed Central文章、覆盖2744种期刊的大规模生物医学导向数据集，融合了文献计量指标、合作特征和通过可复现的三阶段LLM流水线提取的AI参与率，并分析了合作强度与AI参与率对期刊科学影响力的联合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源很少量化合作与AI研究如何共同塑造生物医学领域期刊的声望，缺乏大规模、内容感知的期刊影响力评估框架。

Method: 构建包含文献计量指标、合作特征和LLM派生的AI参与率的数据集；AI参与率通过可复现的三阶段LLM流水线提取；在两个时间子集（2016-2019、2020-2023）上分析合作强度与AI参与率对科学影响力的影响；通过人工评估验证LLM流水线。

Result: 两个主要模式出现：作者团队更大的期刊往往具有更高的引用影响力，而AI参与率仅在2019年子集中与影响因子正相关；人工评估证实了AI相关性检测的实质性一致性和子领域分类的稳定性。

Conclusion: BioMedJImpact提供了生物医学与AI交叉领域的综合数据集以及可扩展、内容感知的科学计量分析验证框架。

Abstract: Assessing journal impact is central to scholarly communication, yet existing resources rarely capture how collaboration and artificial intelligence (AI) research jointly shape venue prestige in biomedicine. We present BioMedJImpact, a large-scale, biomedical-oriented dataset built from 1.74 million PubMed Central articles across 2,744 journals. BioMedJImpact integrates bibliometric indicators, collaboration features, and an LLM-derived AI engagement rate, defined as the proportion of AI-related articles within each journal-year. Specifically, AI engagement rate is extracted through a reproducible three-stage LLM pipeline. We analyze how collaboration intensity and AI engagement rate jointly influence scientific impact across two temporal subsets (2016-2019, 2020-2023). Two main patterns emerge: journals with larger author teams tend to have higher citation impact, while AI engagement rate is positively associated with Impact Factor only in the 2019 subset. To validate the LLM pipeline for deriving the AI engagement rate, we conduct human evaluation, confirming substantial agreement in AI relevance detection and consistent subfield classification. Together, BioMedJImpact provides both a comprehensive dataset at the interface of biomedicine and AI and a validated framework for scalable, content-aware scientometric analysis. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/JonathanWry/BioMedJImpact.

</details>


### [76] [From Trajectories to Evidence: Auditable Experimental Records for Industrial Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05235)
*Zijie Zhuang,Changxin Lao,Pengbo Xu,Hanwen Xu,Ruochen Yang,Yingzhi He,Peng Zhang,Jiangxia Cao,Yusheng Huang,Guohong Mu,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Shuang Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究推荐系统研究智能体中"轨迹到证据转化"问题，提出证据锚定框架，通过受限验证与事后声明限定，将多轮机器学习实验轨迹转化为可审计、可追溯的证据记录，并验证了其在线上部署中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究智能体会在工业推荐场景中开展多轮机器学习实验并保留轨迹用于指导后续决策，但完整轨迹并不自动构成证据：生成产物可能缺乏支持或不完整，执行轮次可能无效或受混淆因素干扰，后续修改可能遮蔽早期结论。因此需要回答"一个已完成的研究过程究竟确立了什么"这一问题。

Method: 提出轨迹到证据转化的证据锚定框架，包含三部分：(1) 上下文隔离的生成-验证-修复过程，在发布前检查产物的证据违规和缺失的下游需求；(2) 事后执行的有效性与归属检查，跨轮次整合证据，将干预级声明限定为可操作的修复、诊断性防护或被搁置的发现，并保留被采纳的声明为带显式溯源和适用边界的可审计记录；(3) 混合LLM辅助控制器根据目标证据对记录进行应用、推迟或拒绝。

Result: 记录审计刻画了哪些声明在限定后存续；下游诊断显示"肯定性适用判断"是所测控制器的瓶颈。在论文到目标的适配中，后期轮次常优于首轮，但最终轮次常劣于较早最优轮，暴露了轨迹演化的非单调性。经完整工作流产出的候选方案相对部署基线获得了正向线上增益。

Conclusion: 轨迹到证据转化框架能够有效地将多轮实验轨迹转化为可信、可审计的证据记录，所产出的方案在线上获得正向提升，但控制器在适用性判断方面仍需改进，且轨迹演化呈非单调特性需要额外关注。

Abstract: Research agents increasingly conduct multi-round machine-learning experiments in industrial recommendation settings and retain the resulting trajectories to guide later decisions. Yet a completed trajectory is not automatically evidence: generated artifacts may be unsupported or incomplete, executed rounds may be invalid or confounded, and later modifications may obscure earlier findings. We study \textbf{trajectory-to-evidence conversion}, asking what a completed research process has actually established. We introduce an evidence-grounded framework that couples bounded verification of consequential artifacts with post-execution claim qualification. A context-isolated generate--verify--repair process checks artifacts for evidence violations and missing downstream requirements before release. After execution, validity and attribution checks consolidate evidence across rounds, qualify intervention-level claims as actionable repairs, diagnostic guards, or withheld findings, and preserve admitted claims as auditable records with explicit provenance and applicability boundaries. A hybrid LLM-assisted controller subsequently applies, defers, or rejects records based on available target evidence. Record audits characterize which claims survive qualification, while downstream diagnostics identify affirmative applicability judgment as a bottleneck for the tested controller. Across paper-to-target adaptations, later rounds often improve on the first, while final rounds frequently underperform an earlier best, exposing non-monotonic trajectory evolution. Candidates produced through the complete workflow also yielded positive online lifts relative to deployed baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-platform epistemic verification for improving factual reliability in AI-generated news summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05302)
*Zhuo Xie,Haoze Ni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MECV（多源证据共识验证）框架，一种事后幻觉修正方法，通过聚合源文档、维基百科和开放网页等多源证据，结合多LLM陪审团机制进行矛盾感知共识评分，对AI生成的新闻摘要中的幻觉内容进行识别与最小编辑修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单通道检索验证无法充分识别AI生成新闻摘要中的幻觉问题，现有方法在事实一致性与语义保持之间难以取得平衡，需要更鲁棒的多源验证机制来提升AI新闻摘要的可靠性。

Method: MECV框架包括三个核心组件：(1) 多源证据聚合——从源文档、维基百科和开放网页检索多个异构证据渠道；(2) 多LLM陪审团机制——通过矛盾感知的共识评分（使用GPT-4o-mini和DeepSeek-Chat作为验证陪审团）估计事实可靠性；(3) 迭代最小编辑修正——对识别为不可支持的声明进行逐步修订。Qwen-Plus作为编排器协调整个流程。

Result: 在SummEdits基准上的实验表明，MECV在提升事实一致性的同时保持了原始摘要的语义结构。实验还发现，异构证据源之间的一致性可以作为识别AI生成摘要中事实不确定性的有效信号，在金融新闻聚合等信息敏感领域同样适用。

Conclusion: MECV为AI新闻摘要中的幻觉修正提供了多源共识验证方案，验证了基于共识的验证方法在提升事实可靠性方面的价值，对可信AI和自动化新闻研究具有贡献。

Abstract: This study proposes Multi-source Evidence Consen- sus Verification (MECV), a post-hoc hallucination cor- rection framework for AI-generated news summariza- tion. Instead of depending on a single retrieval channel, MECV aggregates evidence from multiple heterogeneous sources, including the source document, Wikipedia, and open-web retrieval. The framework further incorporates a multi-LLM jury mechanism that estimates factual reliabil- ity through contradiction-aware consensus scoring across verifier models. Claims identified as potentially unsup- ported are revised through iterative minimal-edit refine- ment. The proposed framework is evaluated on the SummEd- its benchmark using GPT-4o-mini and DeepSeek-Chat as the verifier jury, with Qwen-Plus as the orchestra- tor. Experimental results show that MECV improves fac- tual consistency while preserving the semantic structure of the original summaries. The findings further suggest that agreement across heterogeneous evidence sources can serve as a useful signal for identifying factual uncertainty in AI-generated summaries, including in information- sensitive domains such as financial news aggregation. This study contributes to research on trustworthy AI and automated journalism by introducing a multi-source verification framework for hallucination correction and demonstrating the value of consensus-based verification for improving factual reliability in AI-generated news summarization.

</details>


### [78] [Robustness and User-Perceived Value of Popularity Calibration in Music Recommendation: A User Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05402)
*Oleg Lesota,Gustavo Escobedo,Bruce Ferwerda,Simone Kopeinik,Dominik Kowald,Elisabeth Lex,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究了音乐推荐中流行度校准的感知价值与测量可靠性，发现用户能感知推荐列表的流行度构成差异但并未明确偏好校准列表，且基于JSD的校准指标与用户感知之间的关系受项目熟悉度、列表构成和用户历史可用性等因素影响，计算流行度标签与用户主观判断仅弱对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行度校准研究主要依赖离线指标评估，假设用户偏好与其历史消费分布匹配的推荐列表，但用户研究有限，且校准指标与用户感知的相关性在不同条件下（如项目熟悉度、历史信息不完整）的鲁棒性尚不清楚。作者希望填补这一空白，对流行度校准作为离线指标和用户面向构念进行批判性审视。

Method: 从用户近期收听历史构建个性化曲目列表，使用受控naive推荐器生成三种不同流行度构成的列表：高流行度主导、低流行度主导和校准列表。通过用户实验考察：(1)用户是否能感知列表间的流行度差异；(2)用户是否偏好校准列表；(3)基于JSD的流行度校准在不同熟悉度和历史可用性条件下的鲁棒性；(4)计算流行度标签与用户主观判断的一致性。

Result: 用户能感知列表间流行度构成的差异，但未明确偏好校准列表。JSD与感知流行度之间的关系依赖于项目熟悉度、列表构成和可用用户历史。计算流行度标签与用户自己判断的流行度仅弱对齐。

Conclusion: 流行度校准作为离线指标与用户面向构念的有效性需要更审慎的对待，其测量可靠性受多种条件制约，不应简单假设离线校准指标能直接反映用户偏好或体验。

Abstract: Popularity calibration in recommender systems has been studied both as a form of user-centered personalization and as an indicator of popularity bias. Most existing work evaluates calibration through offline metrics, often assuming that users prefer recommendation lists whose popularity distribution matches their historical consumption profile. However, user studies on calibration remain limited, and existing findings suggest that calibrated recommendations do not necessarily have a strong effect on user experience. Moreover, although prior work has shown that calibration metrics can correlate with users' perceptions of recommendation lists, the robustness of this relation remains unclear under different levels of item familiarity and incomplete user-history information. In this work, we study the perceived value and measurement reliability of popularity calibration in music recommendation. We construct personalized track lists from users' recent listening histories and use a controlled naive recommender to create lists with different popularity compositions: highpop-heavy, lowpop-heavy, and calibrated. We investigate whether users perceive differences between these lists, whether calibrated lists are preferred, how robust JSD-based popularity calibration is under different familiarity and history-availability conditions, and how computational popularity labels align with users' own popularity judgments. Our results show that users perceive differences in popularity composition, but do not clearly prefer calibrated lists. We further find that the relation between JSD and perceived popularity depends on item familiarity, list composition, and available user history, while computational and user-judged popularity labels only weakly align. These findings contribute to a more critical understanding of popularity calibration as both an offline metric and a user-facing construct.

</details>


### [79] [A Mechanistic Analysis of Gender Sensitivity in Dense Retrieval Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05467)
*Catherine Chen,Maarten de Rijke,Carsten Eickhoff*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对稠密检索双编码器模型中的性别偏见进行机制性分析，发现性别信号源自输入嵌入层，并通过一组晚期注意力头（同时携带性别和词匹配信号）传播。基于此发现，作者在输入嵌入层和注意力头两个位置进行介入干预，结果表明嵌入层干预非特异性地中和分数差异，而注意力层干预产生方向性偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究已充分证明稠密检索模型存在性别偏见（男性化文档往往得分更高），但这些偏差产生的内部机制尚不清楚。理解模型内部如何产生性别偏见，是实现精准去偏见干预的前提。

Method: 采用机制性分析方法（mechanistic analysis）对双编码器（bi-encoder）模型进行性别敏感性定位：首先追踪性别信号的来源与传播路径，识别出输入嵌入层及一组晚期注意力头为关键节点；随后在这些定位点分别实施干预（steering interventions）以验证其因果作用。

Result: 性别信号起源于输入嵌入层，并通过少数晚期注意力头（这些头同时编码性别与词匹配信号）传播。输入嵌入层的干预非特异性地中和了性别分数差异，而注意力层的干预则产生方向性的分数偏移，效果截然不同。

Conclusion: 研究为定向去偏见提供了机制层面的依据，同时揭示了性别信号与相关度信号在共享模型组件中难以完全解耦的挑战，说明简单干预难以在不影响检索性能的前提下彻底消除性别偏见。

Abstract: While gender bias in dense retrieval models is well documented, with prior work showing that models often score male-gendered documents higher than female or neutral variants, the internal mechanisms producing these disparities are poorly understood. In this paper, we mechanistically analyze bi-encoder models to localize gender sensitivity, finding that the signal originates in input embeddings and propagates through a small set of late-layer attention heads that carry both gender and term-matching signals. Guided by these findings, we test steering interventions at both identified points and find distinct effects: embedding-level steering non-specifically neutralizes score differences, while attention-level steering produces directional shifts. Our findings provide a mechanistic basis for targeted debiasing and highlight the challenge of disentangling gender from relevance signals in shared model components.

</details>


### [80] [An Ontology-Based Framework for Student Profiling and Content Personalization in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05489)
*José Luiz M. Morais,Arlindo F. da Conceição,Cacilda Encarnação Augusto Alvarenga,Daniela Musa*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过文献研究，探讨在高等教育远程/半远程课程中实施和改进自定步调学习（self-paced learning）与电子学习（e-learning）的关键因素，提出利用本体论（ontologies）存储教育过程信息，并设计了一个面向该目的的计算架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字信息与通信技术的普及以及远程/半远程教育课程的推广改变了教与学过程，要求学生更加自主。然而现有远程课程的教学模式可能未能充分实现学习目标，因此需要识别影响自定步调学习和电子学习实施与改进的关键方面，以重新思考远程课程的教学模式。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，通过文献综述（bibliographic research）分析问题，讨论以电子化、自动化方式监控和记录学习过程与结果的技巧，并强调存储和管理学生画像（profile）（包括内容与访问形式）的重要性。文章进一步提出使用本体论来存储教育过程信息，并为此构建了一套计算架构。

Result: 研究强调了对学习过程与结果进行电子化、自动监控与记录的流程的重要性，并突出了学生画像存储与管理的关键作用。在此基础上，提出了基于本体论的教育过程信息存储方案，并给出了相应的计算架构设计。

Conclusion: 为实现远程高等教育课程更充分的达成学习目标，需要基于学生画像管理和自动化学习监控，采用本体论与计算架构对教育过程信息进行系统化存储，从而为教学模式的重新设计与改进提供支撑。

Abstract: The expansion of access to Digital Information and Communication Technologies and the offer of distance or semi-distance education courses that make use of virtual learning environments brought changes in the teaching and learning processes, requiring that the student be even more protagonist in this process. The present study aimed to identify important aspects to be considered in the implementation and improvement of self-paced learning and e-learning in higher education courses, with the purpose of rethinking pedagogical models of courses offered at a distance so that they reach even more of your learning objectives. The research is characterized as qualitative, of bibliographic nature, and discusses techniques to monitor and record, electronically and automatically, the results of the process and learning. The importance of processes that store and manage the student's profile is highlighted, both in terms of content and forms of access. The article proposes the use of ontologies to store information about the educational process and presents a computational architecture for this purpose.

</details>


### [81] [EXCISE: Query-Side Exclusion for Late-Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05497)
*Mohammed Ali,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EXCISE，一种查询端方法，用于解决延迟交互检索器（如ColBERT类模型）在处理排他性查询（"X但不包含Z"）时的"排他倒置"问题。由于冻结向量无法恢复排他约束，EXCISE通过两个查询端模块（共150万参数）识别排他主题并对100篇候选文档重新嵌入，再用无参数规则降级匹配该主题的候选。在6个数据集和3个骨干模型上，18个组合全部达到最强效果，将ExcluIR上的exclusion success@10从0.058提升到0.691，Boolean NOT准确率从0.25-0.29提升到0.90-0.92，同时不损失nDCG@10。论文还发布了X-BENCH基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟交互检索器在排他性查询（"X但不含Z"）上表现不佳。由于MaxSim评分是加性求和，包含被排除主题Z的文档会被错误地给予高分，作者称之为"排他倒置"。核心难点在于：从冻结的文档向量中无法恢复排他约束，因为识别被排除主题的难度完全取决于查询本身，而非文档。

Method: EXCISE在查询时运行，不修改冻结的索引。具体包括：(1) 一个主题识别模块和一个重新嵌入模块（共150万参数），前者识别查询中被排除的主题，后者对100篇候选文档进行重新嵌入；(2) 一个无参数的规则，用于将匹配被排除主题的候选文档进行降级。整体流程保持了索引冻结，仅在查询端进行干预。

Result: 在6个数据集和3个骨干模型上，EXCISE在所有18个骨干-数据集组合中均优于对应骨干的冻结和微调基线。在ExcluIR上将exclusion success@10从0.058提升至0.691；将Boolean NOT准确率从0.25-0.29提升至0.90-0.92。在1860个查询的汇总中，EXCISE超过了所有微调的交叉编码器，且这些交叉编码器在no-harm nDCG@10上均有损失，而EXCISE在最强的骨干模型上与其冻结基线持平（无损失）。

Conclusion: EXCISE证明排他倒置可以通过纯粹的查询端处理来纠正，无需重新索引或微调检索模型。论文还发布了X-BENCH，一个分层基准，包含显式、隐式和复合排他查询，并提供no-harm和Boolean控制，为未来研究提供了标准化测试框架。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrievers handle exclusion queries poorly. When a user asks for X but not Z, the additive MaxSim score promotes documents covering Z, a problem we call exclusion inversion. We show that no readout of the frozen vectors recovers the constraint, because the difficulty lies in identifying the excluded topic, which depends on the query alone. EXCISE operates at query time and corrects the inversion while leaving the index frozen. Two query-side modules totalling 1.5M parameters identify the topic and re-embed a 100-document shortlist, and a parameter-free rule demotes candidates matching that topic. Across six collections and three backbones, EXCISE is the strongest system in all eighteen backbone-collection cells against that backbone's own frozen and fine-tuned baselines. It raises exclusion success@10 on ExcluIR from 0.058 to 0.691 and raises Boolean NOT accuracy from 0.25-0.29 to 0.90-0.92. Pooled over 1,860 queries, it outperforms every fine-tuned cross-encoder, each of which loses no-harm nDCG@10, whereas EXCISE matches its frozen baseline on its strongest backbone. We release X-BENCH, a tiered benchmark of explicit, implicit, and compound exclusions with no-harm and Boolean controls.

</details>


### [82] [omni-macos: On-Device Omni-Modal Search on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05543)
*Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出omni-macos，一个完全在Mac本地运行的统一多模态搜索引擎，将编码器、索引和存储全部放在用户设备上，文件、查询和向量均不离开机器，并通过内存预算管理、增量重编码、GPU任务调度和量化副本 rescoring 等机制在五台配置差异巨大的Mac上实现了高效本地搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多模态搜索引擎依赖服务器端部署编码器和索引，这导致用户的文件、查询和向量数据必须离开本地设备，带来隐私泄露风险，同时也不适合个人设备上的本地文件检索场景。

Method: 该系统将整个搜索流程（编码器、索引、存储）运行在Mac本地，并实现统一内存预算管理：仅对编辑发生变化的块进行重编码，在用户输入时向GPU分配更小的计算单元，从量化副本回答查询并进行精确重排序，同时将该内存预算传播给基于统一内存的分配器。

Result: 在五台Mac上进行了评测，这些机器覆盖了8倍的加速器宽度差异和32倍的内存差异，每台机器都索引其本地文件，验证了每个机制在不同硬件配置下的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在本地设备上完成整个多模态搜索流程，omni-macos在保护用户隐私的同时实现了跨广泛硬件配置的高效搜索性能，证明了无需服务器的个人设备本地多模态搜索是可行且高效的。

Abstract: A search engine that embeds text, code, documents, images, audio and video into the same representation space has to run its encoder and keep its index somewhere, and almost every component built for the purpose assumes a server. We present omni-macos, which runs that whole engine, encoder, index and store, on the Mac the files are already on, so no file, query or vector ever leaves the machine. It keeps a background indexer and an interactive search box inside one memory budget the user sets: it re-encodes only the chunks an edit changes, hands the GPU smaller units while the user is typing, answers queries from a quantized replica with exact rescoring, and propagates that budget to the allocators that draw on unified memory. We measure every mechanism on five Macs spanning an eightfold range of accelerator width and a thirty-twofold range of memory, each one indexing its own local files.

</details>


### [83] [Is Personalized Modality Weighting Actually Personalized? A Controlled Audit of Per-User Weighting Claims in Multimodal Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05655)
*Jingyuan Zheng,Xin Zhang,Yang Gu,Dongjing Wang,Yuxiang Wang,Xudong Shen,Haiping Zhang,Youhuizi Li,Dongjin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文审计了大规模多模态推荐系统中"每用户模态权重"这一类方法（用户模态强度向量、注意力门控、元权重超网络、低秩引导权重等）。通过双对照审计原则（真实-全局权重对照和真实-洗牌对照），将六种实现统一到共享协同骨干上，跨三个短视频语料和一个跨域电商语料验证发现：单一全局模态权重已获得几乎全部内容增益（+1.9/+3.6/+3.5pp），将其改为逐用户并不能带来一致的效用提升；洗牌对照会高估个性化信号（可达内容增益的128%），其根源在于门控读入了共享协同嵌入。文章提出以real-GM联合real-shuf作为个性化主张的最低证据标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统中大量方法宣称通过每用户模态偏好（模态强度向量、注意力门控、元权重超网络、低秩引导权重等）获得排序增益，但这些方法已在数十亿用户规模上部署。已有评估未将真正用户特定信号与"全局模态权重+模型容量"进行隔离，导致个性化收益可能被系统性高估。

Method: 提出双对照审计原则：（1）real-GM对照——将每用户权重与单一全局模态权重比较，衡量效用差距；（2）real-shuf对照——在评估时置换用户权重绑定，衡量可辨识性差距。将六种实现统一到同一共享协同骨干上进行公平比较，并引入单调信号植入剂量-反应实验（capture AUROC 0.57→0.89、0.64→1.00）验证测试装置能探测若真实存在的用户特定结构。还通过解耦门控输入的消融追踪洗牌膨胀的来源。

Result: 三个短视频语料上，单一全局权重相对于无模态基线已获得接近全部内容增益（+1.9/+3.6/+3.5pp，p<.001）。逐用户权重无一致增益：没有一种实现在所有语料和指标上获胜，少数正收益很小（≤0.9pp）且方向翻转。real-shuf可达内容增益的128%，说明洗牌对照必要但不充分；该膨胀源自门控读取共享协同嵌入，解耦门控输入后real-shuf塌缩至近零而效用结论不变。信号植入验证装置有效，所有结果在第四个跨域电商语料上复制。

Conclusion: 每用户模态权重在个性化上的声称收益在很大程度上可归因于全局模态权重和模型容量，而非真正的用户特定信号。建议将real-GM与real-shuf联合报告作为个性化主张的最低证据标准，并警惕洗牌对照单独使用时的误导性。

Abstract: Per-user modality weighting is deployed at billion-user scale in multimodal recommenders, through user modality-strength vectors, attention gates, meta-weight hypernetworks, and low-rank guided weights, each claiming a ranking gain from user-specific modality preference. Yet, to our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate a genuinely user-specific signal from a global modality weight plus model capacity. We audit this family with a two-contrast audit principle, reducing six implementations onto one shared collaborative backbone and measuring a utility gap (real-GM) against a single global modality weight and an identifiability gap (real-shuf) against an eval-time permutation of the user-weight binding. Across three independent short-video corpora, a single global weight already delivers nearly all of the content gain (+1.9/+3.6/+3.5pp over a no-modality baseline, p < .001). Making the weight per-user adds no consistent utility: no implementation wins on all corpora and metrics, and the few positive gaps are small (<=0.9pp) and flip. The shuffle control is necessary but not sufficient, since real-shuf reaches +128% of the content gain for heads that simultaneously lose to the global weight. We trace this dissociation to gates reading the shared collaborative embedding: decoupling the gate input collapses the inflated real-shuf to near zero while the utility conclusion stands. A monotone signal-implant dose-response (capture AUROC rising from 0.57 to 0.89 and from 0.64 to 1.00) verifies the harness would detect user-specific structure if present, and every finding replicates on a fourth, cross-domain e-commerce corpus. We propose reporting real-GM alongside real-shuf as a minimum evidentiary standard for personalization claims.

</details>


### [84] [Gryphon-v2: One Model in Place of a Cascade - Generate-and-Rank Recommender with Rollout Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06213)
*Anna Lipkina,Daria Tikhonovich,Viktor Yanush,Mariia Ulianova,Oleg Sorokin,Vladislav Dodonov,Ilya Murzin,Denis Burshtein,Nikolay Savushkin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Gryphon-v2，一种统一的生成-排序（generate-and-rank）端到端推荐架构。该模型一次性编码用户历史，用自回归解码器生成Semantic-ID候选，并将其解析为目录项，再通过复用共享编码器状态的item级Ranking Module进行排序。为了将生产环境中的细粒度排序偏好转移到服务路径中而不增加额外模型，作者通过Rollout Distillation将高容量的仅训练用的Teacher Ranker蒸馏到Ranking Module中。在Yandex Music的大规模推荐场景中，单个Gryphon-v2模型替代了包含15+候选生成器、预排序和最终排序的生产级瀑布流系统，活跃用户数提升1.41%，且服务延迟与生产系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常以多阶段瀑布流（多候选生成器、预排序、最终排序）部署，虽然有效但需要重复处理用户历史、复杂的特征管道和多个服务阶段。Semantic-ID的生成式检索提供了更简单的端到端路径，但仅做next-item预测无法捕捉生产排序目标的细粒度偏好。因此需要一个统一架构，既能简化管道又能保留生产级排序质量。

Method: Gryphon-v2采用统一的generate-and-rank架构：(1) 对用户历史编码一次；(2) 用自回归解码器生成Semantic-ID候选；(3) 将Semantic-ID解析为目录项；(4) 通过复用共享编码器状态的item级Ranking Module进行排序。训练采用Rollout Distillation：唯一的排序监督来自Teacher Ranker的分数，并在两种互补的候选分布上收集——当前解码器的rollouts（与serving时生成机制一致）和记录的曝光数据（覆盖用户实际看到的物品），从而在不增加第二个服务模型的情况下转移细粒度生产排序偏好。

Result: 在Yandex Music的大规模推荐表面进行在线A/B实验，单个Gryphon-v2模型替代了包含超过15个候选生成器、预排序和最终排序的生产瀑布流系统。部署后活跃用户数增加1.41%，服务延迟与生产瀑布流系统相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将生成式检索器与从Teacher Ranker蒸馏得到的Ranking Module结合，作为生产瀑布流系统的端到端替代方案，在真实工业场景中具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems are commonly deployed as multi-stage cascades with separate candidate generators, pre-rankers, and final rankers. Although effective, these cascades require repeated user-history processing, complex feature pipelines, and multiple serving stages. Semantic-ID-based generative retrieval offers a path toward simpler end-to-end systems, but next-item prediction alone does not capture the fine-grained preferences encoded by production ranking objectives. We present Gryphon-v2, a unified generate-and-rank architecture for end-to-end recommendation. The model encodes a user history once, generates Semantic-ID candidates with an autoregressive decoder, resolves them to catalogue items, and ranks them with an item-level Ranking Module that reuses the shared encoder states. To transfer fine-grained production ranking preferences without adding an expensive second model to the serving path, we distill a high-capacity, training-only Teacher Ranker into the Ranking Module. Gryphon-v2 is trained with Rollout Distillation: teacher scores are the only ranking supervision, and they are collected over two complementary candidate distributions. Rollouts from the current decoder expose the Ranking Module to candidates produced by the same generation mechanism used at serving time, while logged impressions cover items users were actually shown. In an online A/B experiment on a large-scale recommendation surface at Yandex Music, a single Gryphon-v2 model replaces a production cascade comprising more than 15 candidate generators, pre-ranking, and final ranking. The deployment increases the number of active users by 1.41% at serving latency comparable to the production cascade. These results support the practical viability of a generative retriever with a Ranking Module distilled from the Teacher Ranker as an end-to-end alternative to a production cascade.

</details>

### [85] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Agentic Nesting"的多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自治AI智能体，并组织成分层嵌套的托管拓扑结构，通过统一会话式接口实现跨应用的查询和流程编排，以解决传统企业应用集成（ESB、API网关、RPA）在高耦合、高运维成本和低智能化方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖大量异构业务系统和应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。现有集成方案（ESB中间件、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升和智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，因此亟需一种更高效、更智能的应用编排与集成方法。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting框架：将现有企业应用封装为自治AI智能体，形成分层嵌套的托管拓扑结构（而非平面互联）；从每个遗留应用中提取数字代理（agent proxy）以支持自然语言交互和自主操作；通过中央编排器（orchestrator）进行任务分解和动态调度；对外暴露统一的会话式接口用于跨应用查询与流程编排。同时提出"应用即智能体"（Application-as-Agent）的集成范式和"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）的交互哲学。

Result: 论文的主要贡献包括：提出"Application-as-Agent"集成范式与"Conversation-as-Integration"交互哲学；探索了该方法论在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用等场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting通过分层嵌套的多智能体架构，将企业应用集成的复杂度转化为结构化的智能体协作，有望克服传统集成方案的局限性，为企业级应用编排提供新的解决思路。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [86] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ignition Index (I)指标，通过四参数sigmoid拟合各层线性探测准确率与输入信号强度的关系，提取陡峭度参数beta-hat来量化全局工作空间理论(GWT)的"全或无"点火预测在Transformer语言模型中的表现。在11个模型、5个架构家族上的验证表明该指标具有9.6倍选择性，并揭示了前馈Transformer与SSM、不同架构维度的点火差异等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论(GWT)预测认知处理器中存在"全或无"的点火过渡，但此前缺乏在Transformer语言模型中量化验证这一动态预测的可行指标，需要建立GWT理论预测与机制可解释性之间的定量桥梁。

Method: (1) 对每层线性探针准确率随输入信号强度的变化拟合四参数sigmoid，提取陡峭度参数beta-hat作为点火指标；(2) 使用打乱标签作为对照以排除探针虚假能力；(3) 在11个模型、5个架构家族（含前馈Transformer和SSM）上系统评估；(4) 使用PELT算法检测训练过程中的相变；(5) 通过Mann-Whitney U检验等统计方法验证显著性与效应量。

Result: (1) 前馈Transformer相比SSM在聚合beta-hat上高出89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈近线性轮廓表明缺乏全局广播；(2) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火比深度轴高2.12倍，说明循环架构沿递归维度展示工作空间式转变；(3) Pythia-410M在训练步骤256处检测到PELT相变（+67%），早于归纳头形成；(4) 点火与模型规模和信号强度的假设未获证实。指标具有9.6倍测量选择性（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: Ignition Index是首个经实证验证的、将GWT动力学预测与机制可解释性相连接的定量指标，能够在架构层面区分点火特征，且其对打乱标签的9.6倍选择性证明了测量特异性，为神经语言模型的认知动力学研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [87] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，通过对比强弱模型在相同前缀上的成功与失败局部补丁，构建纠正性教师分布并蒸馏到强模型，从而解决大语言模型在推理任务中因局部推理错误导致的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务上经常失败，但作者认为这些失败并非源于模型整体能力的不足，而是源于中间步骤中的局部推理错误。这些局部错误通常是可修复的——通过插入一个弱探针模型生成的短补丁，就能将推理轨迹导向正确解。然而，直接将弱模型补丁或修复后的轨迹用于微调并不能可靠地将这种纠正效果内化到强模型中，这说明有效信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation框架：1) 在相同前缀下，对比成功与失败的弱模型局部补丁；2) 利用这些对比补丁产生的未来token预测，构建一个纠正性教师分布；3) 将该信号蒸馏到强模型中。这是一个弱到强的训练框架，核心在于从对比性局部干预中学习，而非直接模仿修复文本。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation一致性地提升了强模型的性能，并且优于直接模仿的基线方法。

Conclusion: 推理失败源于局部可修复的逻辑错误，而有效的学习信号在于修复干预对推理分布的重塑效果，而非干预文本本身。对比性局部干预蒸馏是一种有效的弱到强推理能力提升方法。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [88] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中，将连续预测变量替换为临床指南对齐的分类编码是否会在不牺牲性能的前提下提高模型与临床推理的一致性。在多中心欧洲登记数据上，完全分类化的梯度提升模型在两个治疗队列中与连续变量模型统计上无显著差异，尽管一个队列中预测准确率显著下降；全局特征重要性排序保持一致，表明指南导向的分类化是卒中结局模型可行的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中已实现较高的预测准确性，但临床采纳受限，原因是模型解释与临床医生的推理方式不一致。一项临床医生用户研究呼吁采用与临床指南对齐的截断值，促使研究者探索能否在不牺牲预测性能的前提下，用临床信息化的分类编码替代连续预测变量。

Method: 基于多中心欧洲登记数据，将患者按三种治疗方案分为三个队列。使用梯度提升模型，比较标准连续变量模型与完全分类化模型（后者采用卒中指南对齐、治疗特异性的阈值对连续预测变量进行离散化）。通过统计检验评估分类化模型与连续模型之间的预测性能差异，并比较全局特征重要性排序。

Result: 完全分类化的模型在三个治疗队列中的两个里与连续变量模型在统计上不可区分，但在一个队列中预测准确率出现显著下降。全局特征重要性排名保持一致，表明离散化连续预测变量为指南导向的类别能够在所有治疗组中保留预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的分类化是卒中结局模型可行的设计选择，能够在大多数治疗场景下保持预测性能的同时改善模型解释与临床推理的对齐，但在某些特定治疗队列中可能需要权衡一定程度的准确性损失。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [89] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个面向LLM-agent技能复用的多痕迹溯源审计框架。它提取表达（Expression）、实现（Implementation）和操作（Operational）三种溯源痕迹，其中操作痕迹建模为技能操作图（SOG），在审计时对缓存痕迹进行确定性比较并校准。在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上（820个转换后的复用正样本、100个市场锚点、751个负样本）达到AUROC 0.938、F1 0.898，并在36,446个技能的大规模审计中证明其能比仓库级基线更有效地发现可操作的复用审查队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent生态系统围绕可复用技能（混合模态包：元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具、引用和操作工作流）迅速增长。技能成为市场物品后，审计其复用不再等同于普通代码克隆检测。现有检测器只针对单模态源代码或整个包相似性，而技能复用证据分布在多种模态中，可能漏掉仅保留技能某一部分的复用行为。

Method: SKILLTRACE从技能中提取三种溯源痕迹：表达痕迹（Expression）、实现痕迹（Implementation）和操作痕迹（Operational）。操作痕迹被建模为技能操作图（SOG），捕获激活、过程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄取时辅助操作痕迹提取一次；审计时对缓存的痕迹进行确定性比较，针对同功能的严格负样本校准每种痕迹，并报告哪种痕迹支持复用决策。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上，含820个转换后复用正样本（覆盖100个市场锚点）和751个负对照，SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。此外，对36,446个技能的大规模真实世界审计显示，基于痕迹归因的证据能产生可操作的复用审查队列，超越仓库级基线方法。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多痕迹溯源框架有效解决了LLM-agent技能复用审计中跨模态证据分布的问题。实验表明该方法在准确性上显著优于现有基线，并能在大规模真实场景中提供可操作的审计结果，为技能市场的版权和合规监管提供了新的技术支撑。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），一种关系级因果抽象范式，将具体因果对转化为泛化的抽象因果逻辑，并设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳系统（CACI）结合相似性约束聚类从噪声因果数据中提炼可信的AECR，构建两个完整知识库。进一步提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE）将其注入事件预测任务，实验证明AECR显著增强事件因果推理的泛化能力，尤其在罕见和未见事件样本上提升明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重泛化缺陷，导致事件中心智能分析系统（风险预警、决策支持、叙事理解）无法有效利用显式因果知识进行推理。

Method: 1) 提出AECR关系级因果抽象范式，将具体因果对转化为泛化抽象因果逻辑；2) 设计多智能体CACI系统结合相似性约束聚类，从噪声原始因果数据中蒸馏可信AECR，构建两个完整知识库；3) 提出AR-GCAE，通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）基准任务。

Result: 量化实验表明应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，其中在罕见和未见事件样本上增益最为突出。

Conclusion: 关系级抽象因果知识（AECR）相比实例级因果对具有更强的泛化能力，能够有效缓解长尾和未事件组合的因果推理难题，为事件预测系统提供实质性的性能提升。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [91] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个仅15.3M参数的人类国际象棋AI模型，通过将对弈建模为时间感知的序列过程，结合最近20步棋的历史编码与时钟压力调节模块，在6.1亿对局位置（来自1.17亿盘Lichess快棋对局）上训练30天（单张T4 GPU），达到了55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5走子预测准确率，超越此前最优的Maia 2模型，且参数更少、数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统国际象棋走子预测模型将每个局面孤立处理，忽视了人类对弈中时序性和时间压力的影响。作者旨在证明将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动，比仅依赖局面的方法能产生更符合人类行为的走子预测，同时使用更小的模型规模。

Method: Otter采用双条件信号融合机制：(1) 移动历史编码器，基于最近20步棋序条件化预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置漂移和局内行为倾向；(2) 时间控制模块，根据棋钟压力调节预测。模型在1.17亿盘Lichess快棋对局（6.1亿个位置）上训练，仅用单张T4 GPU耗时30天。

Result: Otter在走子预测上达到55.23%的top-1准确率和90.95%的top-5准确率，超越前最优人类棋手模型Maia 2，且参数量和数据量更少。在11个Elo分段（<1100至>=2000）中，1900-1999分段达到峰值57.38%的准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动（而非位置独立的孤立过程）能够以更小的模型获得更符合人类行为的走子预测。同时，代码、训练模型和完整训练日志均已公开释放，便于后续研究复现与改进。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [92] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchAuditBench基准测试和SearchAuditor审计框架，用于评估和提升LLM在深度搜索代理失败轨迹上的定位、归因和修复能力。实验表明最强基线（GPT-5.5）端到端通过率仅26.6%，而SearchAuditor在多种前沿模型上均优于基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率，并能通过修复帮助代理更好地从错误中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理通过长时间跨度的网页交互解决复杂问题，但这一过程既复杂又脆弱——微小的推理错误可能沿长而无噪声的轨迹传播为流畅但错误的答案。诊断这类失败极其困难，需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，这超出了人类的能力范围，因此亟需自动化审计工具来减轻人工负担。

Method: 构建SearchAuditBench基准，包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K tokens），来自8个开放权重模型在5个深度搜索基准上的运行，每条轨迹均经过专家标注关键错误步骤、搜索特定根因和带评分标准的参考修复方案。同时提出SearchAuditor，一种多视角审计框架，通过基于证据的裁决来有效定位、归因和修复搜索代理失败。

Result: 即使最强基线配备GPT-5.5等前沿模型，端到端通过率也仅为26.6%；而SearchAuditor在多种前沿模型上一致优于所有基线，端到端通过率达到32.3%，且利用其修复方案恢复失败运行能使代理更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditor框架在定位、归因和修复深度搜索代理失败方面显著优于现有基线，验证了多视角、证据驱动的审计方法在降低人工审查负担、提升深度搜索代理可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READ（Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search）框架，针对财务报告等长文档中RAG基于chunk嵌入检索的结构性缺陷，改用代理通过三种确定性操作（归一化词法搜索、结构导航、有界跨度读取）在原始文档上直接检索。在780页政府财务报告上，READ以58.8%的准确率远超稠密检索的15.7%，且证明性能提升主要来自"免嵌入接口"而非"智能体迭代"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG对长文档采用"分块-嵌入-取top-k"的标准设计，但财务类文档（财务报表、审计报告、监管报表）具有高度表格化特点：780页政府财务报告中86.8%的内容行是表格行，大量近乎相同的数字在同一嵌入空间中竞争，单位信息来自上方数行的表头，块边界常将数字与其单位（如lakh vs crore）分离，造成两个数量级的误差。这暴露了嵌入检索范式对结构化财务文档的根本性不适用。

Method: 提出READ框架：一个代理通过三种确定性操作读取原始文档——归一化词法搜索（normalized lexical search）、结构导航（structural navigation）和有界跨度读取（bounded span reads），并通过模型上下文协议（MCP）暴露。该方法不依赖Embedding，且每个检索轨迹是可重放的审计轨迹而非不透明的相似度分数。作为对比，构建了表感知chunker（steelman方案）来验证chunking方法仍存在财政年度表头缺失问题。

Result: 在51个验证问题上，READ达到58.8%准确率，远超稠密检索的15.7%（p_Holm=2×10^-5）；与调优后的稠密检索（35.3%）相比，READ仍领先23.5个百分点（p_Holm=0.017）。给智能体相同的循环但仅提供top-k工具时，性能降至27.5%，说明增益来自"免嵌入接口"而非智能体迭代本身。此外，BM25与READ在统计上无显著差异，表明该结果区分的是"基于嵌入"与"免嵌入"检索，而非"智能体"与"词法"搜索。

Conclusion: 对于强表格化的财务类长文档，嵌入式分块检索结构上不健全；READ通过确定性操作+结构感知的接口设计可大幅提升检索准确率。关键洞见是：性能增益主要来自去嵌入化的接口设计，而非智能体循环本身；同时需诚实报告实验边界——READ与BM25无统计差异，说明该工作贡献在于嵌入vs非嵌入之争，而不是智能体vs词法之争。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [94] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS通过将时间对齐的音素标记与连续音频嵌入通过可学习的门控机制自适应融合，解决了3D高斯溅射语音驱动数字人头中的唇部闭合不准和"漏嘴"伪影问题，在HDTF数据集上取得了最佳唇部几何精度（LMD 2.66）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS语音驱动说话人头渲染虽然快速逼真，但在唇部韵律上存在明显缺陷：唇部运动过度平滑，且常违反双唇闭合等硬性发音约束，产生"漏嘴"伪影。根本原因在于从连续声学嵌入中通过回归目标推断短暂、离散的发音事件存在偏差，模型倾向于预测平均化的口型。

Method: 提出PD-GS框架：1）通过ASR和强制对齐流水线获取时间对齐的音素标记；2）设计语言融合模块（LFM），使用可学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在发音关键段强化音素引导，同时保留平滑的音频驱动动态；3）仅用单目视频训练，采用图像重建和唇部标志点监督。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS取得了优于所有对比基线的唇部几何精度（LMD 2.66），并在挑战性音素序列中显著减少了闭合违规现象。

Conclusion: 将离散音素信息融入连续的音频驱动框架能够有效解决回归偏差导致的唇部过度平滑问题，生成更具语言学忠实性的神经数字人。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [95] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，解决特权逐策略蒸馏在多轮智能体中的状态-参考轨迹不匹配问题。通过状态匹配路由筛选可蒸馏的回合，并为匹配状态构建状态条件化教师上下文，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升任务成功率（ALFWorld从0.746到0.865，WebShop从0.574到0.693）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权蒸馏在多轮交互环境中存在根本性缺陷：学生策略的在线轨迹会因先前动作偏离参考轨迹，导致参考轨迹无法覆盖学生实际到达的交互状态，产生状态-参考不匹配。盲目应用特权蒸馏会使教师在学生实际到达的状态下提供不可靠的指导，因此需要显式判断何时以及如何让特权轨迹提供兼容的监督。

Method: 提出SMRC-SD（状态匹配路由与上下文化自蒸馏）。核心包含两个组件：(1) 状态匹配路由（State-Matched Routing）：在每个回合验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的某个已支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态下进行蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的回合；(2) 上下文化自蒸馏（Contextualized Self-Distillation）：对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹中构建状态条件化教师上下文，将监督锚定在学生实际到达的状态上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功全长轨迹蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。消融实验验证了路由选择和状态兼容教师上下文构造两个组件对增益均有贡献。

Conclusion: 显式处理状态-参考匹配是多轮特权蒸馏的关键。SMRC-SD通过选择局部支持的回合和构造状态兼容的教师上下文，有效缓解了状态-参考不匹配问题，显著提升了多轮智能体的任务性能。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [96] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过在Psych-101数据集（1070万条试验级选择数据，来自160个实验）上训练14个从1.35亿到140亿参数的模型（覆盖4个架构家族），研究大规模语言模型作为认知代理时的行为。发现：在分布内，模型规模影响甚微；在分布外，大规模模型泛化能力显著更强。通过逐步剥离提示通道和置换试验顺序的诊断实验，证明模型依赖刺激和反馈内容而非仅凭选择历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调于人类行为数据后可作为通用认知代理，但其规模需求以及模型是真正处理任务结构还是利用统计捷径，仍是悬而未决的问题。作者旨在通过系统性缩放实验和诊断分析来回答这些问题。

Method: 在Psych-101数据集上，从135M到14B参数训练14个模型（4个架构家族），分别评估分布内（留出参与者）和分布外（新颖任务结构）表现。随后对27个实验采用四个诊断：逐步剥离任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈和选择历史四个提示通道，并置换试验顺序以测试对顺序的敏感性。

Result: 分布内：模型性能落入狭窄区间，0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线；分布外：缩放梯度显著变陡，更大模型在新颖任务结构泛化上优势明显。剥离刺激和反馈内容导致75.7%学习信息丢失并使模型低于随机水平，证明选择历史无法解释性能。置换试验顺序显示：独立试验任务不变，而试验顺序由先前响应决定的任务则敏感。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调模型有潜力作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器，但其适用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。模型确实利用了任务结构信息（刺激和反馈）而非仅依赖统计捷径（选择历史）。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [97] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个基于图的、项目级别的自主研究规划层。它将项目brief表示为"创新原子"并组织成有向谱系图，通过轻量级Bernoulli块模型选择水平、垂直或混合的投资组合分解方式，生成具有显式贡献归属、无重叠、依赖感知的任务契约。在十个项目brief（约30个任务）的基准上，其手稿级评估质量得分为7.15，显著优于基准方法（4.58和5.31），与AutoResearchClaw集成后将下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统大多将研究项目视为单个超大任务，产生平坦、模糊且重叠的任务集合，或依赖人工协调任务边界与执行顺序。然而研究项目本质上是长时程议程，需要通过多个有界任务、并行替代方案和依赖感知序列推进。需要一个显式的项目到任务规划层来解决这一缺口。

Method: Project2Task包含三个核心步骤：(1) 给定项目brief，将候选贡献表示为"创新原子"（innovation atoms）并组织成有向谱系图；(2) 使用轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标函数，在水平、垂直和混合三种投资组合分解方式中进行选择；(3) 生成带显式贡献归属的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失的执行字段，输出依赖感知的任务契约，包括目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序。这些契约与具体下游研究执行器无关，支持任务输出整合为连贯的项目级结果。

Result: 在十个项目brief（约30个任务）的基准上：基于手稿的portfolio评估中，Project2Task平均质量得分为7.15，明显优于Brief Baseline（4.58）和Topic-only Setting（5.31）。与AutoResearchClaw集成后，平均下游任务准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、无冗余且可执行的研究任务组合具有明确价值。Project2Task展示了通过图引导的结构化规划，可以显著改善自主研究系统中任务分解的质量和执行效果。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [98] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriQua是一个基于复杂度的自适应事实分解框架，简单声明以标准三元组抽取，复杂声明用超关系事实（附加上下文限定符）表示，并通过TriQuaScore量化事实性，在事实验证和分解质量上优于现有分解式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM事实性评估的"先分解后验证"范式面临根本性权衡：原子事实（一句话承载一条信息）往往丢失必要上下文，而更宽泛的陈述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。现有方法无法在保持原子性的同时保留足够的检索与验证上下文。

Method: TriQua框架根据事实复杂度灵活建模：简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明表示为超关系事实（hyperrelational facts），通过附加辅助上下文限定符（qualifiers）保留关键上下文。验证过程直接在具体三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释性。同时提出TriQuaScore用于量化结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: 实验表明TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性分数高度一致；TriQua实现了稳健的分解质量，并在基于证据的事实验证中优于现有分解式框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过自适应的事实结构设计，在原子性与上下文保真度之间取得了平衡，为LLM事实性评估提供了一种兼具精确性和可解释性的分解-验证方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [99] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"梦景可视化系统"(DSV)，该系统将书面梦境描述转换为时间序列的四格图像。系统先用大语言模型将梦境描述分为四个时间片段，再用文生图模型生成各片段图像，并通过视觉一致性维护及图像重生成机制保证质量。在DreamBank的50个梦境可视化任务上，使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL三个视觉/视觉-语言模型进行客观评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境通常情绪强烈但难以用语言传达，现有文字描述难以充分表达梦境体验。作者希望通过将梦境文本自动可视化为连贯的图像序列，帮助用户更直观地感受和分享梦境。

Method: DSV系统采用两阶段流程：(1) 使用大语言模型(LLM)将梦境描述分解为四个按时间顺序排列的部分；(2) 使用文本到图像(text-to-image)模型为每个部分生成图像，同时在整个序列中保持视觉连贯性。系统还包含重生成机制，对与文本匹配不佳的图像进行重新生成。

Result: 在DreamBank中50个梦境描述的可视化任务上进行了评估，使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL等模型进行客观测量，报告了图像质量、保真度(文本-图像匹配度)和序列连贯性三方面的结果。

Conclusion: DSV系统展示了将梦境文本自动转化为连贯视觉序列的可行性，并建立了基于多模态模型的客观评估框架来量化梦境可视化的质量、保真度和连贯性。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [100] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Search2Skill框架，通过自动识别LLM智能体的能力缺口、搜索外部知识源并蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，实现专家领域中的自我进化。利用基于rubric的强化学习联合优化搜索时机、搜索方式和技能生成，在三个基准的八个专家级领域上优于搜索增强和轨迹式技能学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能方法从模型参数知识或轨迹中内部构建技能，受限于模型已知内容；而专业技能的领域惯例和标准流程往往超出模型知识边界，难以仅从智能体自身获得。因此需要引入外部搜索来弥补能力缺口。

Method: 提出Search2Skill框架，包含三个关键步骤：（1）自动识别智能体的能力差距；（2）搜索外部来源以弥补差距；（3）将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化可复用技能。整个框架通过基于rubric的强化学习方案联合优化"何时搜索""如何搜索"和"如何生成技能"三个决策过程。

Result: 在来自三个基准的八个专家级领域上的实验表明，Search2Skill在流式和留出评估协议下均一致优于搜索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析显示性能提升源于技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且习得的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill验证了通过外部搜索弥补模型知识边界、将证据蒸馏为可复用技能的有效性，技能抽象是性能提升的关键，且技能具备跨模型的可迁移性，为LLM智能体在专家领域的自我进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [101] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LUNAR，首个基于纵向应用交互历史评估LLM跨域行为个性化能力的基准，覆盖衣、食、住、行四大日常生活领域。实验发现行为日志对个性化是必要但不充分的，证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准主要依赖文本人物设定或孤立的单一行为信号，缺乏对跨域行为个性化的系统评估。这种评估不足使得我们难以理解LLM在真实复杂生活场景下如何基于异构的日常活动进行个性化回应，因此需要一个覆盖多领域的纵向行为数据基准。

Method: LUNAR采用多阶段"从粗到细"（coarse-to-fine）的合成流水线，基于真实行为模式构建基准，以缓解数据稀疏和隐私问题。通过对19个主流LLM进行系统实验，评估跨领域行为历史对个性化响应的影响。

Result: 保真度分析显示LUNAR比现有合成基准更接近真实行为分布。实验发现：行为日志是深层个性化的必要但不充分条件；更多上下文或更大模型不保证更好表现；细粒度行为记录的直接检索优于压缩记忆；更强的个性化可能以牺牲隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: 研究识别出证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是个性化LLM面临的三大关键挑战，为未来个性化系统设计提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [102] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体化的、由粗到细的开放世界3D场景生成框架，通过规划智能体将文本提示转化为结构化规格，构建全局连贯的地形基础，并为细节密集区域生成地形条件化组合、重建可编辑纹理网格并恢复其在地形上的放置，最终在保持全局地形结构一致性的同时生成可编辑的实例级资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界具有挑战性，因为系统需要同时维护全局空间连贯性、丰富的局部内容和适合下游编辑和复用的显式资产。

Method: 采用完全智能体化、由粗到细的框架：规划智能体将文本提示转换为区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规格；从语义布局构建全局连贯的地形基础；对细节密集区域生成地形条件化组合、重建可编辑纹理网格、恢复地形放置；渲染智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触。

Result: 在多样的开放世界提示下，WorldClaw生成的大规模场景具有连贯的空间组织、视觉上引人注目的局部内容，以及可编辑的实例级资产，同时保持一致的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw有效解决了开放世界3D场景生成中全局连贯性与局部丰富性的权衡问题，能够同时满足可探索性、视觉质量和可编辑性的需求。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [103] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种场景加权、对抗鲁棒的军事资产态势与可持续性分配（PSA）优化引擎，通过CEV优化器和RobustCEV扩展，在有限时域马尔可夫决策过程框架下解决预部署姿态问题，实验表明相比贪心基线显著提升姿态效率并抵御对抗性威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预部署姿态（pre-commitment posture）是联合作战规划中的关键但长期未解决的正式问题。现有做法依赖贪婪启发式算法，仅最大化价值而忽视地理覆盖，且结构性地易受针对高战略价值目标位置打击的对手影响。需要一种更稳健的优化方法来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 将PSA问题建模为覆盖资产、战区位置和时间步的有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。引入两个优化器：（1）CEV优化器，通过最大化场景加权期望姿态效率来分配资产；（2）RobustCEV扩展，针对一个随观测到部署而更新打击分配策略的贝叶斯对手进行迭代对抗优化。实验在含20个资产和5个战区位置的印太基地环境上进行三项实验验证。

Result: （1）贪心基线因地理覆盖不足产生25.1%的永久姿态效率损失，在价值相关对抗威胁下出现57.3%的场景加权战备度崩溃；（2）CEV优化器在威胁分布含地理信号时比贪心恢复最多19.8%的效率，5到20个精选场景即可捕获大部分增益；（3）RobustCEV扩展面对使用欺骗性威胁先验的适应性对手时，相比朴素优化器恢复最多158%的效率。所有结果通过配对t检验（Bonferroni校正）和两层方差分解验证。

Conclusion: 性能差距是部署策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影，CEV与RobustCEV优化的鲁棒性在对抗性环境中具有显著优势，为军事预部署姿态规划提供了正式的解决方案基础。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [104] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench是用于评测多智能体编排管道故障、恢复与分解的受控基准框架，通过可复现的故障注入与26例金标诊断，揭示了路由策略、恢复层级与故障级联机制，结果表明意图推理路由优于关键词路由，语义类故障几乎不可自动恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体编排基准仅报告任务准确率，而不诊断管道失败的原因、级联的起点或哪次路由决策导致了崩溃。缺乏对失败模式、恢复能力和分解行为的系统化评测手段。

Method: 构建了基于模板化企业流程的、可复现种子的故障注入测试平台。引入级联半径和按失败模式的恢复率作为主要指标。用自举置信区间和配对检验比较路由策略。通过26例金标诊断对比关键词/标志路由器与意图推理路由器，并用Claude智能体在可验证的算术依赖链上做受控机制探针，同时消融研究可信状态修复的作用。

Result: 在对抗性案例上，关键词路由器得分0%，意图推理路由器得分100%（与oracle一致）。五种MAST模式呈现三级恢复：工具故障完全恢复(1.0)、歧义委派部分恢复(0.30)、三种潜在/语义模式完全不可恢复(0.0)。该排序在不同工作流情境和模型（Sonnet/Opus/Haiku）中稳定。盲重试会复现潜在故障并延长探测时间。级联半径随管道深度从0.9增至4.7。消融表明修复增益主要来自可信状态信号而非自主检测。

Conclusion: 检测与归因是故障遏制的前提；当前多智能体系统的自主检测能力不足，需依赖可信状态信号。研究结果定位为受控机制探针而非领域工作流声明，为后续构建更强诊断与修复机制提供基准依据。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [105] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个用于评估大语言模型智能体控制显微镜等科学表征工具的基准测试与轨迹日志框架，系统比较了1/2/3智能体图拓扑、5种LLM、RAG及上下文参数、操作约束等配置在53项显微测试中的表现（共105种配置、1,949次测试、49,109次RAG检索）。结果显示配置间在延迟、token使用、成本和失败模式上存在明显差异，但代理模型无法可靠预测智能体在新任务上的表现，说明现有异构测试套件适用于资格鉴定、回归测试和诊断，但不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正被越来越多地用于控制科学表征工具（如显微镜和同步辐射光束线），但智能体控制物理基础设施的研究仍处于早期阶段，缺乏成熟的智能体系统工程范式。在设计显微镜智能体时需要做出众多选择（LLM类型、智能体数量、职责与委派规则、RAG参数等），研究者既希望智能体能正确执行已知任务，也希望它能泛化到未见过的任务。因此需要一套系统的方法来揭示不同架构选择对性能的影响，以及现有基准在预测新任务泛化能力方面的局限性。

Method: 开发了一个基准测试与轨迹日志框架，用于评估和比较不同的显微镜智能体架构。具体包括：评估一到三智能体的图拓扑结构、五种LLM、RAG与上下文参数以及操作约束；构建了53项显微镜基准测试；系统记录了共105种智能体配置、1,949次测试运行和49,109次RAG检索；并使用代理模型（surrogate models）基于架构和测试结果训练，测试其对新任务的预测能力。

Result: 不同配置在延迟、token使用、成本和失败模式上表现出明显差异。然而，基于智能体架构和测试结果训练的代理模型无法可靠预测智能体在新任务上的表现。这表明当前异构测试套件适用于资格鉴定、回归测试、诊断和直接对比，但不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 该基准框架对于智能体的资格认证、回归测试和故障诊断具有实用价值，能够揭示不同架构配置间的性能差异；但现有基准无法支撑任务无关的全局最优配置选择，研究者仍需针对具体任务进行配置调优，且需要开发更精细的预测方法来评估智能体在新任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [106] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASCADE是一个基于ARACNe调控网络并通过MCP暴露的智能代理框架，用于预测基因扰动的下游转录效应。作者提出了一种新验证方法：用TCGA患者肿瘤数据中焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为敲低逆方向的剂量代理，测试预测方向是否与现实一致，而非仅检查预测基因是否为已知癌症基因。MYC预测在三种癌症类型中显示强一致性，但相比MSigDB基线未见显著超越；扩展至15个额外基因后发现验证具有基因特异性而非普适性。此外，作者单独基准测试了LLM代理是否将自然语言请求正确映射到CASCADE的MCP工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转录效应预测工具的验证方法有缺陷——仅检查预测基因是否为已知癌症基因（成员资格）过于宽松，未检验预测的方向变化是否真实。作者旨在提出更严格的验证范式，通过剂量代理检验预测方向与实际表达变化的一致性，并同时评估LLM代理在MCP工具调用层面的真实可用性。

Method: 利用焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为基因敲低效应的逆方向剂量代理，用真实TCGA病人肿瘤数据验证。对MYC预测目标在BRCA、COAD、STAD三种癌症类型中检验扩增与非扩增肿瘤的表达差异，并进行置换检验基线、PAM50亚型控制、METABRIC独立队列复制、Fisher精确检验对比MSigDB基线。扩展至15个额外基因检验普适性，并单独设计35个查询的基准测试评估LLM代理的MCP工具调用正确性。

Result: MYC预测在三种癌症类型中显示强一致性（BRCA 90.0%, COAD 72.0%, STAD 85.7%，均p<0.0013），显著高于置换基线，通过PAM50控制并在METABRIC中复制（87.2%）。但Fisher精确检验显示CASCADE准确性未显著超越MSigDB公共知识基线。扩展验证发现：增殖机制调控因子大多可复制，而谱系身份转录因子和CCND2一致失败。LLM代理基准测试中，本地模型达71.4%精确匹配（更大模型85.7%），但两个模型在模糊查询上都自信地默认了错误的扰动类型。

Conclusion: CASCADE的基因特异性方向预测在置换基线和朴素随机猜测上表现优异，且MYC预测在真实数据中高度一致；但其准确性未超越现有公共知识库，且模型能力具有基因特异性。LLM代理在MCP工具调用映射上总体表现良好，但存在难以通过针对性修复解决的模糊查询失败模式。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [107] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了GPT-3.5在在线借贷场景中用于反事实分析（预测不同利率方案下的投资回报率ROI）的可行性。研究发现，通过提示工程可将GPT的R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%；GPT在生成反事实ROI时展现出逻辑一致性和因果推理能力，表明LLM在预测与决策领域具有广阔应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析对于决策制定至关重要，但传统方法依赖于复杂机器学习模型。作者希望探索大语言模型（LLM）是否能够在这一领域发挥作用，特别是在在线借贷场景中评估不同利率方案的投资回报率，以提供更灵活、更直观的分析工具。

Method: 作者首先评估GPT-3.5在预测任务上的表现，并与先进的机器学习算法（如梯度提升回归）进行对比；随后利用提示工程（prompt engineering）优化GPT的预测性能；最后让GPT在多种替代利率下生成反事实ROI，检验其逻辑一致性和因果推理能力。
